(&#8220;Xilinx&#8221;) with the Securities and Exchange Commission on March 5, 2021 (the &#8220;Proxy Statement&#8221;), in connection with the merger contemplated by the Agreement and Plan of Merger, dated October 26, 2020, as may be amended from time to time (the &#8220;Merger Agreement&#8221;), by and among the Company, Thrones Merger Sub, Inc., a wholly owned subsidiary of the Company, and Xilinx. Xilinx</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-indent:18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:700;line-height:202%">SILICON VALLEY, Calif. &#8213; April 7, 2021 &#8213; </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">AMD</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%"> (NASDAQ&#58;AMD) and </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">Xilinx, Inc.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%"> </font><font style="color:#0a0a0a;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">(NASDAQ&#58;XLNX) announced today that stockholders voted to approve their respective proposals relating to the pending acquisition of Xilinx by AMD. The acquisition will bring together two industry leaders with complementary product portfolios and customers, combining CPUs, GPUs, FPGAs, Adaptive SoCs and deep software expertise to enable leadership in computing platforms for cloud, edge and end devices. Together, the combined company will have the ability to capitalize on opportunities spanning some of the industry&#8217;s most important growth segments, including data centers, gaming, PCs, communications, automotive, industrial, aerospace and defense.</font></div><div style="text-indent:18pt"><font style="color:#0a0a0a;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">&#8220;For several years, AMD has successfully executed our long-term growth strategy and deepened the company&#8217;s partnerships to drive high performance computing leadership,&#8221; said Dr. Lisa Su, AMD president and CEO. &#8220;The acquisition of Xilinx marks the next leg in our journey to make AMD the strategic partner of choice for the largest and most important technology companies in the world as an industry leader with the vision, talent and scale to support their future innovation.&#8221;</font></div><div style="text-indent:18pt"><font style="color:#0a0a0a;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">&#8220;The Xilinx team is one of the strongest in the industry and we are thrilled to be joining AMD,&#8221; said Victor Peng, Xilinx president and CEO. &#8220;Our shared cultures of innovation, excellence and collaboration </font></div><div style="height:45pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:139%">1</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:45pt;width:100%"><div><img alt="image_11.jpg" src="image_11.jpg" style="height:26px;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:816px"></div><div><img alt="image_11.jpg" src="image_11.jpg" style="height:26px;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:816px"></div><div><font><br></font></div></div><div><font style="color:#0a0a0a;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">will enable us to accelerate growth in the data center and pursue a broader customer base across more markets as a combined company.&#8221;</font></div><div style="text-indent:18pt"><font style="color:#0a0a0a;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">The closing of the transaction remains subject to the satisfaction of other customary closing conditions, including the receipt of required regulatory approvals. The companies continue to expect that the transaction will be completed by the end of the 2021.</font></div><div style="text-align:center"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:700;line-height:202%">About AMD</font></div><div style="text-align:justify;text-indent:18pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">For more than 50 years, AMD has driven innovation in high-performance computing, graphics and visualization technologies &#8211; the building blocks for gaming, immersive platforms and the data center. Hundreds of millions of consumers, leading Fortune 500 businesses and cutting-edge scientific research facilities around the world rely on AMD technology daily to improve how they live, work and play. AMD employees around the world are focused on building great products that push the boundaries of what is possible. For more information about how AMD is enabling today and inspiring tomorrow, visit the AMD (NASDAQ&#58; AMD) </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">website</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">, </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">blog</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">, </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">Facebook</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%"> and </font><font style="color:#0563c1;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%;text-decoration:underline">Twitter</font><font style="color:#0000ff;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%"> </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">pages.</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font style="background-color:#ffffff;color:#171c2d;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:700;line-height:202%">About Xilinx</font></div><div style="text-indent:18pt"><font style="background-color:#ffffff;color:#171c2d;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-weight:400;line-height:202%">Xilinx, Inc. develops highly flexible and adaptive computing platforms that enable rapid innovation across a variety of technologies - from the cloud, to the edge, to the endpoint. Xilinx is the inventor of the FPGA and Adaptive SoCs (including our Adaptive Compute Acceleration Platform, or ACAP), designed to deliver the most dynamic computing technology in the industry. We collaborate with our customers to create scalable, differentiated and intelligent solutions that enable the adaptable, intelligent and connected world of the future.
